No. 60 dated 6 April 2024—The President is pleased to make the following appointment:— 
TERRITORIAL ARMY 
ANTE DATE SENIORITY 

WS-024IM Ex Major Sarbjeet K Gill now TA-43243Y Lieutenant Sarbjeet K Gill has been granted commission as 
an officer in the Territorial Army (Infantry) on 08 May 202] in the rank of Lieutenant under the provision given in Appendix 
जा of Territorial Army Regulations 948 (Revised Edition 976). She is also granted l0 years, (wef 6 Sep 2006 to I5 Sep 
206) ante date seniority with effect from 08 May 20] in the rank of Lieutenant for her former service in the Indian Army.
